- **bridge-pilot**: Only when dispatched by the Bridge Mothership — pilot of the Bridge crew. Implements exactly one slice of the Strategist's DAG — always a tracked Allye task of an approved spec — inside its allowed paths, test-first (red → green through a public seam) when behavior is executable, and returns observed evidence for the Copilot to recheck. Dispatched by the Mothership in launch, mission, repair, optimize and shield; handles at most two correction rounds. ([allye-app/allye-plugin](https://github.com/allye-app/allye-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bridge-pilot.md) · ★1)
- **bridge-recon**: Only when dispatched by the Bridge Mothership — recon of the Bridge crew. Read-only, focused investigation of the repository (and, for cross-app contracts, a sibling app's local clone) to answer one question with evidence — execution paths, invariants, reproductions, tests and seams — without implementing anything. Dispatched by the Mothership in launch, survey, repair, optimize and to research factual questions during blueprint/dispatch interviews. ([allye-app/allye-plugin](https://github.com/allye-app/allye-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bridge-recon.md) · ★1)
- **bridge-reviewer**: 审查 bridge 子系统（Editor WebSocket 客户端 + 游戏运行时桥）改动。聚焦认证、重连、心跳、健康状态机、消息序列化、bridge secret 文件权限。改动 src/core/EditorConnection.ts、src/core/editor-auth.ts、src/core/health-monitor.ts、src/core/reconnection-manager.ts、src/tools/game-bridge.ts 时使用。 ([wgt19861219/godot-mcp-enhanced](https://github.com/wgt19861219/godot-mcp-enhanced/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bridge-reviewer.md) · ★105)
- **bridge-settlement-auditor**: You audit bridge, HTLC, relayer, proof, timeout, lock/mint, burn/release, and settlement logic. ([Cyptopimpinainteazy/x3-atomic-star](https://github.com/Cyptopimpinainteazy/x3-atomic-star/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/bridge-settlement-auditor.md))
- **bridge-shield**: Only when dispatched by the Bridge Mothership — shield of the Bridge crew. Security and correctness reviewer with veto — reviews each slice after Copilot, the stable final diff, and draft specs before submission; runs the `shield` route. Severities CRITICAL..INFO; open CRITICAL/HIGH blocks the slice, the gates and the PR. Issues SHIELD_CLEAR only on the final diff with no open CRITICAL/HIGH and explicit dispositions for the rest. ([allye-app/allye-plugin](https://github.com/allye-app/allye-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bridge-shield.md) · ★1)
- **bridge-specialist**: Bridge-specific specialist. Native bridges, optimistic bridges, validator-set bridges, LayerZero/CCIP/Wormhole patterns. Use whenever cross-chain message-passing is involved. - **bridge-strategist**: Only when dispatched by the Bridge Mothership — strategist of the Bridge crew. Turns an approved Allye spec (anchors, decisions, tasks, Recon's map and the code guide) into a validated DAG of vertical slices — seams, verify commands derived from the repo's own scripts, allowed/forbidden paths, cross-slice contracts and parallel frontiers — and proposes thin tasks when the spec has no tasks, or ACs are uncovered (the Mothership creates them after the user confirms). In `challenge` mode, attacks a draft spec for ambiguities before it is submitted. Runs alone; never implements. ([allye-app/allye-plugin](https://github.com/allye-app/allye-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bridge-strategist.md) · ★1)

Używany wewnętrznie przez umiejętność banana przed każdym wywołaniem generowania obrazu. - **brief-fact-checker**: The final content-correctness gate for a Morning Brief. Given a category + date, reads all four language files and cross-checks every factual claim against its cited source AND an independent corroborating source, plus cross-language fidelity (same fact in DE/EN/PT/ES, no untranslated source-language text leaking into a translation). Use when a routine has finished writing the 4-lang brief and needs it fact-checked BEFORE publish, or when someone says "fact-check the brief", "verify the claims", "is this brief accurate". Read-only — it emits a report and fixes nothing; never logs into sites;… ([danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel](https://github.com/danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-fact-checker.md))
- **brief-fact-checker**: 브리프·릴스 원고·인스타 캡션의 사실 검증 전용. 발행 전 '오류를 찾는 것이 목적'으로 README 체크리스트 1~16과 출처 본문 대조를 PASS/FAIL 로 판정한다. 브리프를 쓴 세션이 아닌 독립 에이전트로 부른다. ([nonojin99/Daily-Card-News](https://github.com/nonojin99/Daily-Card-News/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-fact-checker.md))
- **brief-factory**: Agent de génération de brief éditorial. Analyse la SERP, recherche les mots-clés associés, identifie les gaps concurrentiels, puis produit un brief complet prêt à donner à un rédacteur ou un plugin de rédaction. Déclenché par la command /rankpulse:brief. ([captain-marketing/rankpulse](https://github.com/captain-marketing/rankpulse/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-factory.md) · ★3)
- **brief-fetcher**: Runs 4–6 parallel data fetches to power the Daily Brief skill. Queries HubSpot for cold contacts, overdue tasks, and stalling deals, plus any available connectors (email, calendar, Slack). Returns a single structured JSON payload the Daily Brief skill can inject directly into its artifact. Use only from the Daily Brief skill. For single-contact research, use account-research. ([HubSpot/hubspot-mcp-plugins](https://github.com/HubSpot/hubspot-mcp-plugins/blob/HEAD/HubSpot-Sales/agents/brief-fetcher.md) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **brief-fetcher**: Runs the parallel reads behind the daily-brief skill: undone activities (overdue, due today, upcoming meetings), open deals with no next activity, rotting, closing in 14 days or past their close date, persons with no touch in 14 days, plus any connected email, calendar or Slack. Returns one JSON payload that daily-brief renders. Use only from the daily-brief skill. For research on one person, use account-research. ([pipedrive/pipedrive-skills](https://github.com/pipedrive/pipedrive-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-fetcher.md) · ★1)
- **brief-fidelity-reviewer**: Isolated grader of a decision brief against the memorandum it was built from. Judges whether the brief says what the memo says — no new claim, no dropped condition, no shifted certainty, no open point presented as settled, no high-risk conclusion left out. ([gregmos/memoforge](https://github.com/gregmos/memoforge/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-fidelity-reviewer.md) · ★43)
- **brief-filler**: Use this agent to fill in a corporate creative brief template (production or tender) from an existing project brief. Triggers on "заполни бриф", "сделай ТЗ на креатив", "бриф на производство макетов/роликов", "подготовь бриф для подрядчика". Writes ONLY into the template's value cells, preserving fonts, kegl, table structure and styles byte-for-byte. Output — .docx next to the source project. ALWAYS asks the user for the volume and count of ad materials before building. ([arbort/growth-marketing-skills](https://github.com/arbort/growth-marketing-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-filler.md))

- **brief-researcher**: Researches v1 Meridian source code and yggdrasil design docs to assemble evidence packs for brief authoring. Use when gathering v1 file paths, types, function signatures, module structures, or cross-references between DESIGN.md sections, CHECKLIST items, and USER-STORIES for a specific brief or brief range. Does not write briefs — produces a structured research artifact that brief-writer consumes. Invoke per brief, not per cluster. - **brief-researcher**: Gather live, dated, cross-checked source material for ONE Morning Brief category on ONE date, and write it to a research dossier the brief-writer consumes. Runs on a cheaper model because research is fetch-heavy and low-judgement — it collects sourced facts, it does NOT select stories, write prose, forecast, or do ELI5. Use as the research phase of the consolidated morning-brief routine, before the writer. ([danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel](https://github.com/danieldeusing/morning-briefs-daniel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-researcher.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that runs exactly the review brief it is dispatched with: a per-concern criteria slice, a downstream blast-radius search, a spec comparison, a restatement batch, or a pull request risk-map check. Dispatched by /review:quality-gate (slice, code-design, downstream, spec, close-out and restatement modes), /review:fanout and the /review:fanout-sweep workflow (criteria slices), /review:council (council members), and /review:explain-change; not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★32)
- **brief-reviewer**: Statically reviews a design brief or requirement (DECISION-BRIEF.md, a spec's Problem/Context section, or an equivalent requirement doc) for clarity, completeness, and testability before it hardens into a spec. Read-only -- cannot edit the brief. ([dwarvesf/dwarves-kit](https://github.com/dwarvesf/dwarves-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★14)
- **brief-reviewer**: Review a draft product brief against leadership review patterns. ([akshatk7/ai-product-development-os](https://github.com/akshatk7/ai-product-development-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews a drafted yggdrasil implementation brief against the canonical brief template and the source design docs. Use after brief-writer produces a brief draft, when verifying that every CHECKLIST item claimed as realised is actually realised by ≥1 R#, every USER-STORY claimed as satisfied is concretely addressed, no R# lacks acceptance criteria or file paths, and no dangling prerequisite-brief references. Returns a structured verdict — does not edit the brief. ([ablative-io/beamr](https://github.com/ablative-io/beamr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **brief-reviewer**: Challenge the draft brief against code, tests, history, and user-facing behavior. Flag inferred intent presented as fact, implementation accidents presented as requirements, contradictions omitted from the narrative, and acceptance criteria that cannot be verified. ([batteryshark/skill-tap](https://github.com/batteryshark/skill-tap/blob/HEAD/skills/productivity/reverse-brief/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: You are the **Brief Reviewer** for MicroHard. ([Imad-Oute/MicroHard](https://github.com/Imad-Oute/MicroHard/blob/HEAD/src/departments/coo/team-ops/mh-coo-briefing-protocol/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: Grades a past NAS100 trading day's brief against what price actually did, and reports whether the model needs tuning. Runs in the BACKGROUND after a brief has already been delivered — it must never delay or alter the brief itself. ([PravinderSamra/CTrader-Bots](https://github.com/PravinderSamra/CTrader-Bots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews one case brief against its decision before /case-brief saves it. Sends back an item of law that is not the court's own voice at its pinpoint, ratio and obiter told apart wrongly, a part that breaks the template's shape, and anything of the working matter the brief names or states, and a Treatment block its citing decisions do not bear out. Reports when the decision is in the working matter's own litigation, which is never briefed. Returns strict JSON. Read-only: writes nothing and states no law. Dispatched by /case-brief, once per brief it returns. ([Mharbulous/probanda-dev](https://github.com/Mharbulous/probanda-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/researcher/agents/brief-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **brief-reviewer**: Independently and skeptically critiques a just-confirmed Project Brief against caller-supplied success criteria (the bar), optionally checking its claims against caller-named context files, and reports a per-criterion verdict, ranked pushback, and one-line fixes. Strictly read-only. Used as the second-AI check in the interview skill's step 7, and available to any other flow that needs an outside read of a scope/brief document before work starts. Not for rewriting the brief, designing the solution, or auditing the codebase — critique only. ([CommanderBosko/NixOS](https://github.com/CommanderBosko/NixOS/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/bosko/claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Operator-invoked pre-spec review of a brief ('brief-reviewer: &lt;path&gt;'): a Codex grounding round, then a ChatGPT right-thing-to-build round. Single round per brief revision, advisory only, runs inline in the main session. ([michaelhazza/sectool](https://github.com/michaelhazza/sectool/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brief-reviewer.md))
- **brief-reviewer**: Reviews the product brief against the brief rubric and reports pass or fail for each check, with evidence. Started by the brief skill with `Document: &lt;path&gt;` as its request's first line. To review a brief, use the brief skill rather than starting this agent yourself: the skill runs the whole review sequence. - **brief-reviewer**: Stand-in for the brief reviewer in the failed-check evals. Always reports one failure the drafting agent can correct, so every run reaches the correction check. ([michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/michaelmarconi/agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/evals-failed-check/agents/brief-reviewer.md))

Keeps docstring format declarations, cross-references, and the landing page's feature list in sync with the actual public API as it evolves. Stops to ask the user whenever the spec is ambiguous or a test uncovers a genuine implementation bug, rather than guessing or silently working around it. Must support every brim_version the spec currently defines — discovered fresh from the spec repo, never hardcoded — via a lazy-loaded per-version strategy registry rather than duplicated functions or scattered if/elif version branches. Stops to ask the user whenever the spec is ambiguous or a change reveals a genuine bug in already-shipped validation logic. - **browser-ext-reviewer**: ブラウザ拡張機能のコード変更をChrome/Firefox両対応の観点でレビューする専門エージェント。 以下の状況で使用: (1) ユーザーが「ブラウザ拡張のコードをレビューして」「拡張機能のコード変更をチェックして」と依頼した時 (2) ユーザーが「Chrome/Firefox互換性をチェックして」「クロスブラウザ対応を確認して」と依頼した時 (3) ブラウザ拡張のPRレビュー時にクロスブラウザ互換性を確認したい時 (4) manifest.jsonの変更を含むコミットを検証したい時 ([goldeneggg/dotfiles](https://github.com/goldeneggg/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai-linux/deprecated/agents/browser-ext-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **browser-extension**: Chrome Extension + CDP expert for packages/browser-extension. Use for CDP protocol, chrome.debugger API, manifest configuration, popup/background communication, and website capture logic. ([lomidze1/Web2Wireframe](https://github.com/lomidze1/Web2Wireframe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/browser-extension.md))
- **browser-extension-builder**: Use this agent to build, maintain, or debug the NeuroDock browser extension. Owns the WXT-based Manifest V3 codebase for Chrome, Firefox, and Edge. Active heavily in Phase 2. Responsible for content script integration with Gmail, Slack, Linear, Notion, GitHub, Google Docs, and Outlook web. ([tlennon-ie/neurodock](https://github.com/tlennon-ie/neurodock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/browser-extension-builder.md) · ★20)
- **browser-extension-engineer**: Use when you need to build browser extensions with constrained permissions and reliable messaging. ([crankyjediii/claude-code-subagents](https://github.com/crankyjediii/claude-code-subagents/blob/HEAD/categories/07-specialized-domains/agents/browser-extension-engineer.md))
- **browser-extension-implementer**: Use to build ONE task of a browser-extension-solution-designer plan: the worker handlers, content scripts, extension UI, typed messages and storage it names, plus Vitest fake-chrome tests. Do NOT use without a task + contract, to redesign or verify, or for web Angular (web-angular-implementer). ([envoydev/alfred-code](https://github.com/envoydev/alfred-code/blob/HEAD/stack/agents/browser-extension-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **browser-extension-reviewer**: Review browser/client boundaries, DOM/origin behavior, extension privileges, messaging, storage and browser-only build/runtime exposure. Produce candidates only. ([omarmohelal/SecHelix](https://github.com/omarmohelal/SecHelix/blob/HEAD/agents/browser-extension.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **browser-extension-solution-designer**: Use when a browser-extension (MV3) feature needs designing before code: a read-only pass that settles the worker, content-script and UI topology, storage, message contract, permissions and cross-browser strategy, then splits parallel tasks. Do NOT use to write code or for a web Angular app. ([envoydev/alfred-code](https://github.com/envoydev/alfred-code/blob/HEAD/stack/agents/browser-extension-solution-designer.md) · ★1)
- **browser-extension-testing**: Use this procedure when a change touches Browser, the extension, or the shared browser protocol. Build and run the app and extension from the same worktree. An extension installed from `main` does not verify a feature worktree. ([facundoPri/link-log](https://github.com/facundoPri/link-log/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/browser-extension-testing.md))
- **browser-extension-verifier**: Use once every browser-extension-implementer task has landed: a read-only gate over the assembled extension against the plan and TypeScript quality that reruns the build, the Vitest and E2E suites and returns a per-task punch-list. Do NOT use to fix, or for web Angular (web-angular-verifier). ([envoydev/alfred-code](https://github.com/envoydev/alfred-code/blob/HEAD/stack/agents/browser-extension-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **browser-fallback-agent**: Playwright browser automation agent handling Microsoft portal operations that lack CLI or API coverage, with screenshot verification ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/tvs-microsoft-deploy/agents/browser-fallback-agent.md))
- **browser-fast**: __NAME__'s cheaper helper for READING and COMPARING on websites with nothing sensitive (prices, listings, product pages, public information), on a smaller model. Not for forms, errands, sites where __OWNER__ is signed in or payments: those go to browser. ([jonjunq/claudia](https://github.com/jonjunq/claudia/blob/HEAD/templates/workspace/.claude/agents/browser-fast.md) · ★1)
- **browser-fetcher**: Background agent for fetching web content using **agent-browser CLI**. ([actionbook/actionbook](https://github.com/actionbook/actionbook/blob/HEAD/playground/rust-learner/agents/browser-fetcher.md) · ★1.6k)
- **browser-fetcher**: Background agent for fetching arxiv.org web content using **agent-browser CLI**. ([actionbook/actionbook](https://github.com/actionbook/actionbook/blob/HEAD/playground/arxiv-viewer/agents/browser-fetcher.md) · ★1.6k)
- **browser-fetcher**: Generic web content fetcher. ([actionbook/rust-skills](https://github.com/actionbook/rust-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/browser-fetcher.md) · ★1.6k · 4 copies)

- **browser-first-local-app-engineer**: Use when you need to build local-service plus browser UI apps with health gates, logs, launchers, and shutdown. ([FluxonLab/Skillry](https://github.com/FluxonLab/Skillry/blob/HEAD/plugins/mobile-desktop/agents/browser-first-local-app-engineer.md) · ★10)
- **browser-fixer**: Analyzes browser screenshots and console errors to diagnose and fix UI issues. Used by shipwright-build (browser verify), shipwright-test (E2E fix mode), and shipwright-iterate (F0.5 web runner). ([svenroth-ai/shipwright](https://github.com/svenroth-ai/shipwright/blob/HEAD/plugins/shipwright-test/agents/browser-fixer.md) · ★14)
- **browser-frame**: The frame around every browser-check brief (`docs/agents/subagents.md` § Supervision). - **browser-harness-mac**: &gt; **Scope guard — read this first.** This applies **only on the local macOS workstation**, where &gt; browser-harness drives the **Brave-Clawdia** profile over local CDP on port 9222 (e.g. capturing &gt; screenshots for the blog). It does **not** apply on any Linux or pod clone (the secure-agent-pod, retired 2026-11, was one) of &gt; this repo: there, browser-harness uses the **remote Browser Use cloud bro ([derio-net/frank](https://github.com/derio-net/frank/blob/HEAD/agents/browser-harness-mac.md) · ★1)
- **browser-hypothesis-tester**: ZNTEQR browser red-team runner for a single canvas case (RT-xx). Idempotent per case ID. ([phan-tan-7211/ZNT-EQR](https://github.com/phan-tan-7211/ZNT-EQR/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/browser-hypothesis-tester.md))
- **browser-integration**: Use for TypeScript work - Chrome and Firefox extensions, DOM inspection, browser-IDE communication, source mapping, framework adapters and browser security. - **browser-investigator-subagent**: You are a browser investigation specialist that handles verbose web automation tasks while keeping the parent agent's context clean. - **browser-lane-census**: What the 70 Playwright suites actually do when something runs them [POD-1227]. ([madeinorbit/podium](https://github.com/madeinorbit/podium/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/browser-lane-census.md) · ★51)
- **browser-locator**: Locates routes, screens, and DOM-level component presence in a running web application via the Playwright executor. Call browser-locator when you need to enumerate WHERE things appear in the rendered UI, not to extract their detail. ([atomicinnovation/accelerator](https://github.com/atomicinnovation/accelerator/blob/HEAD/agents/browser-locator.md) · ★35)
- **browser-login-handoff**: Playwright MCP・claude-in-chrome でブラウザ操作する複数のスキル(person-state 系の private スキル含む)で、対象アカウントが未ログインだったときの ハンドオフ手順をその場で都度組み立てていた。ユーザーから「この手順、 コモディティ化(型化)したい」というフィードバックを受けた(#562)。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/browser-login-handoff.md) · ★1)
- **browser-mcp**: Use this when a task needs real-browser verification of the app: driving pages, taking screenshots, reading console/network output, profiling, or debugging layout and behavior. ([civictechdc/votecatcher](https://github.com/civictechdc/votecatcher/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/browser-mcp.md) · ★3)
- **browser-mcp**: This repo registers the [Playwright MCP server](https://github.com/microsoft/playwright-mcp) at **project scope** in `.mcp.json` (checked into git), so anyone — human or agent — working in this repo gets it automatically once they approve it. ([richardhcli/akasha](https://github.com/richardhcli/akasha/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/browser-mcp.md))
- **browser-media-maker**: Opens the user's Chrome (Claude in Chrome tools) or the built-in browser and asks a web AI tool where Bu Nasser is already signed in - Gemini, ChatGPT, Higgsfield, Kling, or another he names - to generate an image or a video from a detailed English prompt the main Claude wrote, then waits, checks the result against the prompt and reports where it is. - **browser-mobile-tester**: Use to test the running app in a real browser via Playwright. Drives the app served on port 8081, always in a mobile-phone screen size (iPhone, 390x844). Invoke whenever you need to navigate the app, click/type/interact, inspect the page, take screenshots, or verify a UI flow works end-to-end in the browser. ([dhomini-rabelo/ben-prototype](https://github.com/dhomini-rabelo/ben-prototype/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/browser-mobile-tester.md))
- **browser-mode-tester**: Agent specialized in testing the Flutter web app using Playwright (browser E2E tests) and Flutter unit/widget tests. REQUIRES a valid app-plan.md from the planning-agent before starting any work — rejects tasks if the plan is missing. When called by flutter-coding-agent: responds directly with results and does NOT call the coder back. When acting autonomously: may call flutter-coding-agent to report bugs. Reads test instruction markdown files produced by other agents, or tests the whole app autonomously when no instructions are provided. ([Padlboy/flutter_template_project](https://github.com/Padlboy/flutter_template_project/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/browser-mode-tester.md))
- **browser-navigator**: Autonomous browsing agent for multi-step web tasks. - **browser-operator**: Does multi-step work on the page open in the browser tab of the person's right pane — fills a form, goes through a flow, checks what a page shows. Give it the goal and what it may and may not do; it reports what it did and saw. Use it instead of calling the browser_* tools one by one. The extension is optional: with no connection the agent honestly returns an "unavailable" report. 🔴 LAUNCH GOTCHA: if more than one Chrome is connected, the main session MUST pass a ready `deviceId` in the pro… ([mosu1985/vibe-flow](https://github.com/mosu1985/vibe-flow/blob/HEAD/plugins/vibe-flow/agents/browser-operator.md) · ★5)
- **browser-operator**: Investigate and operate browser-based workflows while keeping external actions visible and approval-gated. ([Leevity/Ola](https://github.com/Leevity/Ola/blob/HEAD/resources/agents/browser-operator.md) · ★4)
- **browser-operator**: Haiku 5.5 browser operator. Send it a multi-step web job — reach a page, fill a form, read a value off a dashboard, check a flow end to end — and it drives playwright-cli itself and hands back the result in a few lines, so no page snapshot or screenshot enters the caller's context. Isolated browser by default; the user's own logged-in Chromium only when the brief says the task needs their login. The brief must say the goal, what counts as done, and every text it may type and every commit action (submit, send, pay, delete…) it may take; anything not authorised there it stops and hands back. ([saifulapm/dotfiles](https://github.com/saifulapm/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/dot_claude/agents/browser-operator.md) · ★3)
- **browser-operator**: Browser worker on Sonnet for work in a web browser that is not testing the user's own app: vendor and account setup, a dashboard to read or configure, a form to fill. Spawned by the /browser skill with a brief (goal, site and account, which browser, values to enter, limits, proof wanted); drives the user's signed-in Chrome or a clean browser, stops at steps only the user can take, and returns a report with screenshots.
